### Reference

- [Verified] Abdelmessih, K. (n.d.). *Moontower*. <https://moontower.substack.com/> (accessed 2026-11) — ★ · Options and volatility intuition from a former options trader: why vol is the traded asset, how market makers think about edge and risk. Backs [Volatility risk premium and vol arb](../docs/02-strategies/f-volatility-and-derivatives/volatility-risk-premium-and-vol-arb.md) · [Options market making](../docs/02-strategies/a-liquidity-provision/options-market-making.md). — *cited in:* [09-reference/talks-podcasts-blogs.md](../docs/09-reference/talks-podcasts-blogs.md)
- [Verified] Alpha Architect (n.d.). Blog. <https://alphaarchitect.com/blog/> (accessed 2026-11) — ★★ · Accessible reviews of academic factor and anomaly papers. The firm also issues factor ETFs, so weigh the framing. Backs [Factor investing fundamentals](../docs/02-strategies/e-risk-premia-and-factors/factor-investing-fundamentals.md). — *cited in:* [09-reference/talks-podcasts-blogs.md](../docs/09-reference/talks-podcasts-blogs.md)
- [Verified] Ang, A. (2014). *Asset Management: A Systematic Approach to Factor Investing*. Oxford University Press. <https://doi.org/10.1093/acprof:oso/9780199959327.001.0001> (accessed 2026-11) — ★★ · The textbook case for treating factors, not asset classes, as the unit of allocation. Strong on value, momentum, low-vol and the economics of each. Backs [Factor investing fundamentals](../docs/02-strategies/e-risk-premia-and-factors/factor-investing-fundamentals.md) · [Value, quality and low volatility](../docs/02-strategies/e-risk-premia-and-factors/value-quality-low-vol.md). — *cited in:* [09-reference/books.md](../docs/09-reference/books.md)
- [Verified] AQR Capital Management (n.d.). Research library. <https://www.aqr.com/Insights/Research> (accessed 2026-11) — ★★ · Working papers and journal articles on value, momentum, carry, defensive and trading costs. Many factor papers in the [Papers](../docs/09-reference/papers.md) list originate here. Backs [Factor investing fundamentals](../docs/02-strategies/e-risk-premia-and-factors/factor-investing-fundamentals.md) · [Momentum](../docs/02-strategies/e-risk-premia-and-factors/momentum.md). — *cited in:* [09-reference/talks-podcasts-blogs.md](../docs/09-reference/talks-podcasts-blogs.md)
- [Verified] arXiv (n.d.). Quantitative Finance: Trading and Market Microstructure (q-fin.TR), recent submissions. <https://arxiv.org/list/q-fin.TR/recent> (accessed 2026-11) — ★★★ · Where microstructure, execution and market-making preprints appear first. Preprints are unrefereed. Backs [Order-book and trade-flow signals](../docs/03-algorithms/f-market-making-and-hft-algorithms/order-book-and-trade-flow-signals.md). — *cited in:* [09-reference/talks-podcasts-blogs.md](../docs/09-reference/talks-podcasts-blogs.md)
- [Verified] Asness, C. (n.d.). *Cliff's Perspectives*. AQR Capital Management. <https://www.aqr.com/Insights/Perspectives> (accessed 2026-11) — ★ · A founder's essays, often on value's drawdown, factor timing and critics of quant. Read them as the strongest statement of one side of [contested debates](../docs/08-calibration/contested-debates.md). Wiley. <https://doi.org/10.1002/9781119571278> (accessed 2026-11) — ★ · Short essays by WorldQuant researchers on mining many weak signals at scale. Read Turnover, Alpha Correlation, Backtest – Signal or Overfitting?, Controlling Biases and Machine Learning in Alpha Research. Backs [Feature engineering](../docs/03-algorithms/a-data-and-signals/feature-engineering.md) · [Signal transforms](../docs/03-algorithms/a-data-and-signals/signal-transforms.md) · [Overfitting and multiple testing](../docs/03-algorithms/c-validation-and-backtesting/overfitting-and-multiple-testing.md). — *cited in:* [09-reference/books.md](../docs/09-reference/books.md)
- [Reported] Two Sigma (2018). "Two Sigma: Using News to Predict Stock Movements." Kaggle competition. <https://www.kaggle.com/competitions/two-sigma-financial-news> (accessed 2026-11) — ★★ · Market data plus licensed news analytics, from the pre-LLM era. Useful for seeing how hard news signals are to monetize. Backs [NLP and LLM signals](../docs/02-strategies/d-mid-frequency-alpha/nlp-and-llm-signals.md). — *cited in:* [09-reference/datasets-and-tools.md](../docs/09-reference/datasets-and-tools.md)
- [Verified] Two Sigma (n.d.). Insights and articles. <https://www.twosigma.com/insights/> (accessed 2026-11) — ★★ · Data-science and factor-risk articles, including material on its factor-lens framework. Backs [Risk models](../docs/03-algorithms/d-portfolio-construction/risk-models.md) · [Factor investing fundamentals](../docs/02-strategies/e-risk-premia-and-factors/factor-investing-fundamentals.md). — *cited in:* [09-reference/talks-podcasts-blogs.md](../docs/09-reference/talks-podcasts-blogs.md)
- [Verified] U.S. SEC (n.d.). EDGAR application programming interfaces (XBRL company facts and submissions on data.sec.gov). <https://www.sec.gov/search-filings/edgar-application-programming-interfaces> (accessed 2026-11) — ★★ · Free JSON of every XBRL financial fact, each stamped with its filing date and accession number, so you can rebuild what was reported and when. **What goes wrong:** XBRL tags vary across firms and years. The SEC throttles clients that do not declare a user agent, and full-text NLP still needs the filings themselves. Backs [Fundamental quant signals](../docs/02-strategies/d-mid-frequency-alpha/fundamental-quant-signals.md) · [NLP and LLM signals](../docs/02-strategies/d-mid-frequency-alpha/nlp-and-llm-signals.md). — *cited in:* [09-reference/datasets-and-tools.md](../docs/09-reference/datasets-and-tools.md)
- [Verified] U.S. Securities and Exchange Commission (2005). "Regulation NMS." Final rule, Release No. 34-51808. <https://www.sec.gov/rules/final/34-51808.pdf> (accessed 2026-11) — *cited in:* [09-reference/glossary.md](../docs/09-reference/glossary.md)
- [Verified] Wharton Research Data Services (n.d.). WRDS. <https://wrds-www.wharton.upenn.edu/> (accessed 2026-11) — ★★ · The gateway most academics and many funds use for CRSP, Compustat, TAQ, OptionMetrics, I/B/E/S and more. **What goes wrong:** access is by institutional subscription, and academic licences generally bar commercial use. Code written against WRDS tables does not run elsewhere. Backs [Point-in-time data and cleaning](../docs/03-algorithms/a-data-and-signals/point-in-time-data-and-cleaning.md). — *cited in:* [09-reference/datasets-and-tools.md](../docs/09-reference/datasets-and-tools.md)
- [Verified] Zuckerman, G. (2019). *The Man Who Solved the Market: How Jim Simons Launched the Quant Revolution*. Portfolio/Penguin. <https://www.penguinrandomhouse.com/books/557104/the-man-who-solved-the-market-by-gregory-zuckerman/> (accessed 2026-11) — ★ · A history of Renaissance Technologies. Read it for culture and history. Every firm detail in it is journalism, not disclosure, so treat it as `[Reported]`. Backs [Stat arb: overview and history](../docs/02-strategies/c-statistical-arbitrage/stat-arb-overview-and-history.md) · [Evolution of quant strategies](../docs/02-strategies/h-cross-cutting/evolution-of-quant-strategies.md). — *cited in:* [09-reference/books.md](../docs/09-reference/books.md)
